**P0.3: production migration row matches the file**
1. **Expected checksum:** `git show origin/main:prisma/migrations/20260913120000_kyc_at_withdrawal/migration.sql | sha256sum`. Hash the **git blob**, never the checked-out file (core.autocrlf adds CRLF) and never through a PowerShell pipe.
2. **Connect:** `railway login` (Ali approves the pairing), then `railway link` to 50pick/production. Use the Postgres service's `DATABASE_PUBLIC_URL`.
3. **Query, read-only:** `select migration_name, checksum, finished_at, rolled_back_at from _prisma_migrations where migration_name like '%kyc_at_withdrawal'`.
   - **GO** only if the checksum equals step 1's LF hash **or** its CRLF variant (`git show origin/main:prisma/migrations/20260913120000_kyc_at_withdrawal/migration.sql | sed 's/$/\r/' | sha256sum`; Prisma stores the hash of the bytes on the machine that applied it), `finished_at` is set and `rolled_back_at` is null. Record which variant matched.

**P0.6: local admin render**
1. `npm run build`, then `DATABASE_URL=<the URL npm run db:scratch prints> DISABLE_ADMIN_TOTP=true npm run start` (the start script runs `prisma migrate deploy` first and needs `DATABASE_URL`), with a seeded owner.
2. Open `/admin` and read the screenshot.
3. Record "phase D = next start: WORKS / NOT MEASURED". `next dev` for admin stays forbidden (`03-design-spec.md` §7) unless an amendment changes it.

## Build: 8 commits (PLAN §11; amendment placement per `04-amendments.md`)

**Definition of done, identical for every commit:**
- **impl:** implemented.
- **tsc:** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean.
- **suites:** the commit's new `test:` suites pass.
- **all:** `npm run test:all` green, with a server on `:3000` (`npm run build`, then `DATABASE_URL=<db:scratch URL> npm run start`).
  - Without a server, skip the two server suites (see `scripts/test-all.mjs` header for the exact `--skip` names) and record them NOT MEASURED.
- **red:** its `red:` harness passes, with each mutation failing its own assertion.
- **drive:** real behaviour driven or rendered as the commit requires, with screenshots opened and read.
- **review:** the adversarial review workflow ran with 3 lenses. Reviewers write their answer once at the end, and every confirmed finding is fixed.
- **docs:** `docs/HOUSE-BOTS.md` and this file updated.
- **push:** `house-bots` pushed (branch confirmed with `git branch --show-current` first).

### Scope per commit
Detail lives in `PLAN.md` + `04-amendments.md`. Names here are pointers, not specs.

**Commit 1: docs of record, schema, migrations, DAL, pure modules**

- **Schema + 2 migrations:**
  - S1 migration law.

- **Code:** DAL + in-memory twin; `src/lib/house-bot/{constants, pause-reasons, rules, clock}`; `server/house-bot/book.ts`.

- **Suites:** `test:house-bot-rules`, `test:house-bot-migrations`, `test:dal-parity`.
- **N1/N2 schema and pure modules:**

**Commit 8: end-to-end and release prep**
- ⛔ **D20 (Ali, 2026-09-17):** L26 and L27 are no longer house-bots release preconditions (no house CSV column ships); they stay live platform defects for Ali. The final docs follow D20: reports treat house accounts as ordinary player accounts.
- ⚠️ **THE TWO KEY NAMES WERE CORRECTED 2026-09-20, everywhere at once** (`seed:house-bots-local` → `db:seed-house-bots-local`, `drive:house-bots-local` → `qa:house-bots-local`). Measured: `package.json` has exactly three `db:seed-*-local` keys and **no `drive:` namespace at all**, and every key pointing at a `*-drive` file is `qa:` (`qa:house` is the nearest sibling). A plan that keeps naming a command nothing answers to is the rot `test:docs` and `test:guards-exist` both exist to stop.
- **Ops scripts:** `db:seed-house-bots-local`, `qa:house-bots-local`, `ops:preflight-house-bot-migrations` (A23), and the Commit-8 DUTIES of `ops:house-bots-status` — the file itself shipped in Commit 7 (see the note there), so this list owes its rollback figures (open house positions, live intents), the per-market exposure, the WALLET_MISSING condition and the +10 min recheck, not the command.
  - **✅ `ops:preflight-house-bot-migrations` BUILT 2026-09-20 (branch `ops-lane`), with `test:house-bot-ops` §6.** Read-only; nothing typed that can be derived — the 8 tables and 7 marker columns are IMPORTED from `schema-ready.ts` (the module the engine's own gate and `/api/health` read), the two migration FOLDERS and the FIVE index names are read off disk, and the markers migration is identified by the only property that defines it (it is the house migration that adds all seven marker columns), so no timestamped folder name is typed either.
    - ⭐ **The timezone line is a VERDICT, not a print** (04 A23 only asked for a print). A non-UTC database lets BOTH migrations apply cleanly and the engine then refuses to start on every replica, silently — `pre.3` walks a scratch database to `Africa/Dar_es_Salaam` and the preflight says NO-GO; `pre.9z` proves the verdict is not a constant by agreeing with the zone an untouched database really has.
    - ⭐ **`indisvalid`, not `indexname`** — a failed `CONCURRENTLY` build leaves an INVALID index under the SAME name and `IF NOT EXISTS` keeps it. `pre.5` makes one invalid in the catalogue (NO-GO naming it) and `pre.5b` makes it valid again (GO returns), so the NO-GO is attributable.
    - **⛔ REL-2's `--post` branch is deliberately NOT built, recorded as a decision in the file's header:** REL-2 is struck for this release by events (both migrations are already applied on `main`), and a flag that writes DDL to production would turn the one command safe to run mid-incident into one that is not. A future house migration gets its own script, never a flag on a preflight.
    - Floors MEASURED by running it: **memory 66, postgres 80** (+8 / +11). Red controls: **42 planted, 42 SEEN RED** including the plan's own four-index miscount as code, the by-name index check, and the eight table names re-typed.
    - ⛔ §6 sits PHYSICALLY BEFORE §5 in the case file though it is numbered after it: the sunset empties the roster and nothing may follow it.
    - **NOT MEASURED, with its reason:** the production row counts and sizes — the preflight's whole reason for existing — are barred from this programme (ruling 553) and are read at REL-0 with Ali's go. Every OTHER path is exercised on scratch Postgres, before and after `migrate deploy`, on a database §6 creates and drops itself.
  - **✅ THE COMMIT-8 DUTIES OF `ops:house-bots-status` BUILT 2026-09-20, with `test:house-bot-ops` §7.** Four figures beyond R5's five, all from ONE invocation (an officer mid-rollback who must run two commands to answer "is it safe yet" will run one of them): **6** open house positions (marked, OPEN, every bot, all time — ENG-21's step 2), **7** live intents, **8** open exposure PER MARKET with its TZS total and market count (FS-06's "TZS X across N markets"), **9** the settlement-blocked condition (HB-LC-10 / WALLET_MISSING). Plus `--watch`, PLAN §12's "recheck 0 marked rows after 10 minutes" made a command.
    - ⛔ **There is NO global live-intent reader in the DAL** (`countLiveForTarget` and `listLiveOnMarket` are both scoped), so the statuses are IMPORTED from `LIVE_INTENT_STATUSES` and the suite additionally pins that the DAL's own `LIVE_SQL` names EXACTLY those two — the count and the seam's live reads cannot drift apart.
    - ⛔ **Figure 9 is a READ and only a read.** The planner's 7e pass AUTO_PAUSEs such an account; a status command run mid-incident must not move the desk under the officer reading it. The suite asserts the account was untouched after the condition fired.
    - ⛔ **Drift is NAMED at every invocation.** Without `--drift` it prints NOT MEASURED and the command that measures it — a clean five-figure line must never be readable as a clean drift verdict.
    - ⭐ **Both new refusals have positive controls, and both were measured:** the holder wallet is DELETED (condition reported, account untouched) and PUT BACK (condition clears on the same account); and `--watch`'s delta detector is proved by placing a house stake BETWEEN its two reads, so "nothing moved" is a measurement rather than a constant.
    - ⚠️ **Two assertions were written wrong and the suite caught both** — recorded because they are the lane's own failure mode: a blanket "every figure names its population" reddened on figures that are not counts (a switch state, a boot instant, a beat age), and a detector hunting the bare word `AUTO_PAUSED` reddened on figure 2's own printed population LABEL. Both are now scoped, and the second keeps the false positive as a control of its own.
    - **✅ `db:seed-house-bots-local` BUILT 2026-09-20, with `test:house-bot-ops` §8.** `scripts/seed-house-bots-local.mts` — the world a human OPENS: two polls with real titles, a player holding a real stake, and four accounts, one per status the desk can show. It is assembled from `loadWorld()` and differs from it in exactly the three places that matter: every account is designated through `designateHouseBot` and started through `startHouseBot` (the fixture designates through the DAL and always ends ACTIVE), and the AUTO_PAUSED one gets there through a REAL password change and the REAL holder hook — `seed.2` proves its recorded consent fingerprint no longer matches its holder's password, because a status poked into a column would seed a world the product cannot produce and the first thing a human learned from it would be false.
      - ⛔ **It is NOT run through `db:scratch --run`**, and the header says why: that mode stops the cluster when its command exits, so a seed wrapped in it would appear to work and leave nothing behind for the browser to open.
      - ⛔ **It cannot turn the master switch on** (`seed.7`), refuses an un-migrated database through the engine's OWN schema gate rather than a query of its own (`seed.0`), and refuses to seed a SECOND roster silently on top of the first (`seed.again`).
      - ⚠️ **Two defects this lane wrote and the controls caught.** (1) `seed.6` first read as "it mentions 127.0.0.1 and names production" — which passes on a file that asks the right question and CARRIES ON; it is a triple now (host AND production AND `process.exit(2)`), with the test-but-run shape planted red. (2) The admin phone and password were TYPED here, copied from `seed-admin-local.mts`: right the day it was written, and a dead password the day that file changes either half. They are READ OUT of that seed's own output now, `seed.4` tries the PRINTED credential against the stored hash, and `seed.4c` — one character more, the same verifier says no — sits beside it.

**REL-2: migrations applied to production from the build machine**
1. **Pre-check:** `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 DATABASE_URL=<Postgres DATABASE_PUBLIC_URL> npx prisma migrate status` shows exactly the 2 house migrations pending.
2. **Apply:** `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 DATABASE_URL=<Postgres DATABASE_PUBLIC_URL> npx prisma migrate deploy`.
3. **Verify:** both rows finished, and each row's checksum equals `git show HEAD:<migration file> | sha256sum` or its CRLF variant (`… | sed 's/$/\r/' | sha256sum`), whichever matches the bytes on this machine (`git ls-files --eol <file>`); record which. A checksum mismatch alone is NOT a failed apply; never `migrate resolve --rolled-back` a finished row.
4. **On failure:** follow the S2 recovery (preflight `--post`, then `migrate resolve --rolled-back`). Never leave a failed row.
5. ⚠️ **This is an explicit exception** to the 50pick-audit skill's "migrations reach production only through the deploy". It is needed because the start script applies DDL while the old container still serves. It must be named in Ali's REL-0 "go" and recorded in the COMPLIANCE entry.

**REL-4: merge and deploy**
1. Read the live service config and record `overlapSeconds`. An outage is expected only if it is null; the switch stays OFF regardless.
2. Merge and push from a **temporary release worktree**. Never use the main checkout another session may be in; the house-bots worktree's pre-push guard stays armed.
   1. `git -C <repo> fetch origin`
   2. `git -C <repo> worktree add --detach <repo>-rel4 origin/main`
   3. `git -C <repo>-rel4 merge --no-ff --no-edit origin/house-bots`
   4. `git -C <repo>-rel4 push origin HEAD:main`: the one sanctioned `main` refspec in this whole programme. **This is the deploy.**
   5. `git -C <repo> worktree remove <repo>-rel4`

   If step 3 conflicts, stop, remove the release worktree and re-run REL-0; never resolve a conflict at the deploy gate.

**REL-5: post-deploy checks, within 10 minutes**
- `curl -s -D - -o /dev/null https://50pick.tz/ | grep -io 'dpl=[0-9a-f]*' | head -1 | cut -d= -f2` equals `git rev-parse origin/main`.
- `/api/health` shows `ok:true` and `leadership.lifecycle.isMe=true` — and **names nothing about house bots** (C5-SPEC ruling 171: no `houseBots` key, no `house-bot` lease). The house schema state is read on `/admin/system` → Diagnostics as ADMIN ("Ready") (ruling 172). ~~`houseBots.schemaReady=true`~~ (superseded by D19, ninth session).
- After the 90 s boot grace, `ops:house-bots-status` shows: OFF, 0 bots, 0 marked rows, engine enabled, beats fresh.
- PLAN §12 production checks and §15 phase F pass.
- **At +10 min:** 0 marked rows and 0 HOUSE_BOT notifications.

⛔ **From REL-4 on, record REL-5, REL-6 and every later change to this file on branch `house-bots` only.** A docs-only push to
`main` is another production deploy.

## Rules for updating this file
1. **Same commit as the work.** Change Status now, RESUME AT, and the rows you touched in the commit that did the work. Push `house-bots` immediately.
2. **Never rebase `house-bots`.** It is shared across machines. Bring in `main` with `git merge --no-edit origin/main`; take other machines' work with `git merge --ff-only origin/house-bots`, or `--no-edit` if both sides have commits.
3. **Stopping mid-commit** (end of session, machine trouble): commit what exists as `WIP house-bots: <what>`, write the exact state into RESUME AT (done / half-done / next command), push. WIP commits are fine on this branch.
4. **git is the truth for code.** Record commit *subjects* and dates here, not SHAs as facts (`git log` has them).
5. **Never quote a recorded number as current** (counts, balances, ids); re-derive it and say when.
6. **A new machine** follows `README.md` steps 0–5 for tools, Postgres, the Railway login and the push guard. Secrets are not in git.
7. **At the end of every session**, add one Session log row (date · `hostname` · paths · what happened · where it stopped), even if nothing shipped.
